<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> parseDraft</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">draft</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">column</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> EditableColumn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> undefined</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> EditableValue</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (column?.type </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!==</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "number"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> draft</span></span>

<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * Spreadsheet-style table on the virtualized Table core. Cells edit in place</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * (double-click, Enter or just type; Enter/Tab commit and move, Esc cancels),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * arrow keys walk the grid, and border handles insert or delete rows and</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * columns: the grip on a row's left edge and the chevron on a header open</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * menus, and the + pills on each border insert right there. Everything glides</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * into its new place.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> */</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /* focus management ------------------------------------------------------- */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  // Scrolling waits a frame: when a row was just inserted, the virtualizer only</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  // knows the new count after React commits. Scrolling to it earlier clamps to</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  // the old last row and the virtualizer's reconcile pass then snaps the</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  // viewport back, so every "New row" click jolted the table.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> focusCell</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> React.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">useCallback</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">pos</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> CellPos</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">reveal</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "auto"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "end"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "auto"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">    setActive</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(pos)</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> blankRow</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> ()</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> EditableRow</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">        createColumn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">cols</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> ({ key: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">`note${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">cols</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">length</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, header: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"Notes"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, width: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">160</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, placeholder: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"—"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> })</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span></span>
